Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..7)
find y: int(0..3)
find z: int(0..4)

such that

x % y % z != 3

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#rank_1: bool
  find x#rank_2: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#rank_2),and([!(x#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

y
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
y
new variables:
  find y#rank_1: bool
  find y#rank_2: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(y#rank_2),or([!(y#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#rank_1: bool
  find z#rank_2: bool
  find z#rank_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(z#rank_3),and([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

x
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(5, 7)]), [x#rank_1,x#rank_2;int(1..)] [5, 7])

--

y
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(0, 3)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2;int(1..)] [0, 3])

--

z
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(0, 4)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 4])

--

!(z#rank_3)
   ~~> apply_tseytin_not ([("SAT", 9005)])
__0
new variables:
  findAux __0: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__0 <-> !(z#rank_3)) using tseitin

--

!(x#rank_2)
   ~~> apply_tseytin_not ([("SAT", 9005)])
__1
new variables:
  findAux __1: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__1 <-> !(x#rank_2)) using tseitin

--

!(z#rank_2)
   ~~> apply_tseytin_not ([("SAT", 9005)])
__2
new variables:
  findAux __2: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__2 <-> !(z#rank_2)) using tseitin

--

!(x#rank_1)
   ~~> apply_tseytin_not ([("SAT", 9005)])
__3
new variables:
  findAux __3: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__3 <-> !(x#rank_1)) using tseitin

--

!(z#rank_1)
   ~~> apply_tseytin_not ([("SAT", 9005)])
__4
new variables:
  findAux __4: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__4 <-> !(z#rank_1)) using tseitin

--

and([__3;int(1..)])
   ~~> remove_unit_vector_and ([("Base", 8800)])
__3

--

and([__4;int(1..)])
   ~~> remove_unit_vector_and ([("Base", 8800)])
__4

--

or([__1,__3;int(1..)])
   ~~> apply_tseytin_and_or ([("SAT", 8500)])
__5
new variables:
  findAux __5: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__5 <-> or([__1,__3;int(1..)])) using tseitin

--

and([__2,__4;int(1..)])
   ~~> apply_tseytin_and_or ([("SAT", 8500)])
__6
new variables:
  findAux __6: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__6 <-> and([__2,__4;int(1..)])) using tseitin

--

or([__0,__6;int(1..)])
   ~~> apply_tseytin_and_or ([("SAT", 8500)])
__7
new variables:
  findAux __7: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__7 <-> or([__0,__6;int(1..)])) using tseitin

--

SATInt(Rank([(5, 7)]), [x#rank_1,x#rank_2;int(1..)] [5, 7]) % SATInt(Rank([(0, 3)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2;int(1..)] [0, 3]) % SATInt(Rank([(0, 4)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 4]) != 3,
__5,
true,
__7
   ~~> remove_single_atom ([("SAT", 8400)])
SATInt(Rank([(5, 7)]), [x#rank_1,x#rank_2;int(1..)] [5, 7]) % SATInt(Rank([(0, 3)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2;int(1..)] [0, 3]) % SATInt(Rank([(0, 4)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 4]) != 3,
true,
__7
new SAT encoding decisions:
  assert(__5) using tseitin

--

SATInt(Rank([(5, 7)]), [x#rank_1,x#rank_2;int(1..)] [5, 7]) % SATInt(Rank([(0, 3)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2;int(1..)] [0, 3]) % SATInt(Rank([(0, 4)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 4]) != 3,
true,
__7
   ~~> remove_single_atom ([("SAT", 8400)])

true
new SAT encoding decisions:
  assert(__7) using tseitin

--

   ~~> mod_to_bubble ([("Bubble", 6000)])
